# ABI-031 progress

- Status: Done

## Execution plan

- [x] motion-fit-preflight: Manager reproduces the deployed jerk and decoration-size failures, freezes frame timings, transform-delta bounds, family fit ratios, affected owners, and browser evidence matrix

- [x] continuous-hit-repair: Fix the smallest shared animation owner so reactions interpolate continuously from the current pose and return without snap, replay, accumulation, or family-specific forks
- [x] decoration-fit-repair: Reuse profile and semantic-anchor ownership to fit decoration scale, clearance, and orientation to each family and boss without adding a second layout system
